>>
>>     Write-Output "`n--- SCANNING: $root ---"
>>
>>     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $root -PathType Container)) {
>>         Write-Output "STATUS: DIRECTORY NOT PRESENT"
>>         $scanSummary += [PSCustomObject]@{
>>             Root = $root
>>             Status = "Not present"
>>             Matches = 0
>>             Errors = 0
>>         }
>>         continue
>>     }
>>
>>     $scanErrors = @()
>>
>>     $found = @(
>>         Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $root -File -Recurse -Force `
>>             -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue `
>>             -ErrorVariable +scanErrors |
>>         Where-Object { $_.Name -match $pattern }
>>     )
>>
>>     Write-Output "Matches: $($found.Count)"
>>     Write-Output "Access/scan errors: $($scanErrors.Count)"
>>
>>     $scanSummary += [PSCustomObject]@{
>>         Root = $root
>>         Status = "Scanned"
>>         Matches = $found.Count
>>         Errors = $scanErrors.Count
>>     }
>>
>>     foreach ($f in $found) {
>>
>>         $record = [PSCustomObject]@{
>>             Path = $f.FullName
>>             Name = $f.Name
>>             Length = $f.Length
>>             LastWriteTime = $f.LastWriteTime
>>             Extension = $f.Extension
>>             Version = ""
>>             SHA256 = ""
>>             Signature = ""
>>         }
>>
>>         if ($f.Extension -eq ".dll") {
>>
>>             $version = [System.Diagnostics.FileVersionInfo]::GetVersionInfo(
>>                 $f.FullName
>>             )
>>
>>             $record.Version = $version.FileVersion
>>
>>             $record.SHA256 = (
>>                 Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Algorithm SHA256
>>             ).Hash
>>
>>             $signature = Get-AuthenticodeSignature -FilePath $f.FullName
>>
>>             $record.Signature = $signature.Status
>>         }
>>
>>         $allMatches += $record
>>
>>         Write-Output "`nFILE: $($record.Path)"
>>         Write-Output "SIZE: $($record.Length)"
>>         Write-Output "MODIFIED: $($record.LastWriteTime)"
>>         Write-Output "VERSION: $($record.Version)"
>>         Write-Output "SHA256: $($record.SHA256)"
>>         Write-Output "SIGNATURE: $($record.Signature)"
>>     }
>> }
